<script>
const socket = io();
let currentUser = null;
let nodes = {}, stars = [];
let zoom = 1, panX = 0, panY = 0;
let dragging = false, dsx = 0, dsy = 0;

const canvas = document.getElementById('starry-sky');
const ctx = canvas.getContext('2d');
const COLORS = { self:'#30d158', wifi:'#0a84ff', bluetooth:'#64d2ff', peer:'#bf5af2', spore:'#ff375f', ble:'#5ac8fa', cellular:'#ffd60a', industrial:'#ff9f0a', super:'#ffffff' };
const RANK = { self:0, wifi:1, super:2, spore:3, ble:4, bluetooth:5, cellular:6, industrial:7, peer:8 };

function toggleSidebar() { document.getElementById('sidebar').classList.toggle('open'); }
function toggleInfoPanel() { document.getElementById('infoPanel').classList.toggle('hidden'); }

function resize() {
  canvas.width = window.innerWidth;
  canvas.height = window.innerHeight;
  if (!panX && !panY) { panX = canvas.width / 2; panY = canvas.height / 2; }
}
resize();
window.addEventListener('resize', resize);

canvas.addEventListener('wheel', e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  const d = e.deltaY > 0 ? 0.9 : 1.1;
  const nz = Math.max(0.2, Math.min(5, zoom * d));
  const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
  const mx = e.clientX - r.left, my = e.clientY - r.top;
  const wx = (mx - panX) / zoom, wy = (my - panY) / zoom;
  zoom = nz;
  panX = mx - wx * zoom;
  panY = my - wy * zoom;
}, { passive:false });

canvas.addEventListener('mousedown', e => { dragging = true; dsx = e.clientX - panX; dsy = e.clientY - panY; });
canvas.addEventListener('mousemove', e => { if (dragging) { panX = e.clientX - dsx; panY = e.clientY - dsy; } });
canvas.addEventListener('mouseup', () => dragging = false);
canvas.addEventListener('mouseleave', () => dragging = false);
canvas.addEventListener('dblclick', () => { zoom = 1; panX = canvas.width / 2; panY = canvas.height / 2; });

function hash(id) {
  let h = 0;
  for (let i = 0; i < id.length; i++) { h = ((h << 5) - h) + id.charCodeAt(i); h = h & h; }
  return Math.abs(h);
}

function layout(nd, pm) {
  const h = hash(nd.node_id);
  const a = (h % 360) * Math.PI / 180;
  if (nd.type === 'self') return { x:0, y:0 };
  if (nd.type === 'wifi') { const r = 170 + (100 - (nd.signal || 50)) * 2.2; return { x:Math.cos(a)*r, y:Math.sin(a)*r }; }
  if (nd.type === 'spore') { const p = pm[nd.parent] || { x:0, y:0 }; const r2 = 45 + (h % 25); return { x:p.x + Math.cos(a)*r2, y:p.y + Math.sin(a)*r2 }; }
  if (nd.type === 'bluetooth' || nd.type === 'ble') { const r3 = 100 + (h % 40); return { x:Math.cos(a)*r3, y:Math.sin(a)*r3 }; }
  if (nd.type === 'cellular') { const r5 = 220 + (h % 50); return { x:Math.cos(a)*r5, y:Math.sin(a)*r5 }; }
  const r4 = 260 + (h % 60);
  return { x:Math.cos(a)*r4, y:Math.sin(a)*r4 };
}

function animate() {
  ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,4,40,0.15)';
  ctx.fillRect(0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
  if (Math.random() < 0.06) {
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,' + (Math.random() * 0.5) + ')';
    ctx.fillRect(Math.random() * canvas.width, Math.random() * canvas.height, 1.5, 1.5);
  }
  ctx.save();
  ctx.translate(panX, panY);
  ctx.scale(zoom, zoom);
  ctx.lineWidth = 1 / zoom;
  const pm = {};
  stars.forEach(s => pm[s.node.node_id] = s);
  stars.forEach(s => {
    const p = s.node.parent && pm[s.node.parent];
    if (p) {
      const col = s.node.type === 'spore' ? 'rgba(255,55,95,0.4)' : 'rgba(10,132,255,0.25)';
      ctx.strokeStyle = col;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.moveTo(p.x, p.y); ctx.lineTo(s.x, s.y); ctx.stroke();
    }
  });
  stars.forEach(s => {
    s.pulse += 0.04;
    const r = s.base * (1 + Math.sin(s.pulse) * 0.22);
    const col = s.node.evolving ? '#ffd60a' : (COLORS[s.node.type] || '#8888aa');
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(s.x, s.y, 0, s.x, s.y, r * 5);
    g.addColorStop(0, col + '80');
    g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, r * 5, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = g; ctx.fill();
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, r, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = col; ctx.fill();
    if (s.node.type === 'self') { ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(s.x, s.y, r * 0.4, 0, Math.PI * 2); ctx.fillStyle = '#fff'; ctx.fill(); }
    ctx.font = (11 / zoom) + 'px -apple-system,sans-serif';
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
    ctx.fillText(s.node.label || s.node.name || s.node.node_id, s.x + r + 6 / zoom, s.y + 3 / zoom);
  });
  ctx.restore();
  requestAnimationFrame(animate);
}
animate();

function addLog(msg, type) {
  const c = document.getElementById('logContainer');
  const e = document.createElement('div');
  e.className = 'log-entry ' + (type || 'info');
  e.textContent = '[' + new Date().toLocaleTimeString() + '] ' + msg;
  c.appendChild(e);
  while (c.children.length > 200) c.removeChild(c.firstChild);
  c.scrollTop = c.scrollHeight;
}
function clearLogs() { document.getElementById('logContainer').innerHTML = ''; }

socket.on('connect', () => {
  document.getElementById('statusDot').classList.add('on');
  document.getElementById('statusText').textContent = 'Подключено';
  addLog('Соединение установлено', 'success');
  socket.emit('request_stats');
});
socket.on('disconnect', () => {
  document.getElementById('statusDot').classList.remove('on');
  document.getElementById('statusText').textContent = 'Отключено';
  addLog('Соединение потеряно', 'error');
});
socket.on('server_log', d => { addLog(d.msg, d.level === 'ERROR' ? 'error' : (d.level === 'WARNING' ? 'warn' : 'info')); });
socket.on('inbox_new', m => { addLog('Новое сообщение от ' + (m.sender || '?'), 'success'); loadInbox(); });
socket.on('nodes_update', d => {
  nodes = d.nodes || {};
  const pm = {};
  const order = Object.values(nodes).sort((a, b) => (RANK[a.type] || 9) - (RANK[b.type] || 9));
  order.forEach(nd => { pm[nd.node_id] = layout(nd, pm); });
  stars = order.map(nd => {
    const old = stars.find(s => s.node.node_id === nd.node_id);
    const p = pm[nd.node_id];
    return { node: nd, x:p.x, y:p.y, base:(nd.type === 'self' ? 7 : 3 + (nd.trust || 50) / 25), pulse: old ? old.pulse : Math.random() * 6 };
  });
  document.getElementById('statNodes').textContent = Object.keys(nodes).length;
  document.getElementById('statInfected').textContent = d.infected || 0;
});
socket.on('stats_update', st => {
  document.getElementById('statSent').textContent = st.packets_sent || 0;
  document.getElementById('statDelivered').textContent = st.packets_delivered || 0;
  document.getElementById('statFailed').textContent = st.packets_failed || 0;
  const u = st.uptime || 0;
  document.getElementById('statUptime').textContent = Math.floor(u / 60) + 'м ' + Math.floor(u % 60) + 'с';
});

document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(b => {
  b.addEventListener('click', () => {
    document.querySelectorAll('.tab-btn').forEach(x => x.classList.remove('active'));
    b.classList.add('active');
    const t = b.dataset.tab;
    document.getElementById('loginForm').style.display = t === 'login' ? 'block' : 'none';
    document.getElementById('registerForm').style.display = t === 'register' ? 'block' : 'none';
  });
});

function showAuth() { document.getElementById('authScreen').style.display = 'flex'; document.getElementById('mainInterface').style.display = 'none'; }
function showMain() {
  document.getElementById('authScreen').style.display = 'none';
  document.getElementById('mainInterface').style.display = 'block';
  document.getElementById('currentUsername').textContent = currentUser.username;
  document.getElementById('currentUserNode').textContent = currentUser.node_id || '';
  loadContacts(); loadInbox();
}
function checkAuth() {
  fetch('/api/me').then(r => r.json()).then(d => { if (d.success) { currentUser = d.user; showMain(); } else showAuth(); }).catch(showAuth);
}
function doLogin() {
  const u = document.getElementById('loginUsername').value.trim();
  const p = document.getElementById('loginPassword').value;
  fetch('/api/login', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({username:u, password:p}) })
  .then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    if (d.success) { currentUser = d.user; showMain(); addLog('Добро пожаловать, ' + u, 'success'); }
    else { document.getElementById('authError').textContent = d.error; }
  });
}
function doRegister() {
  const u = document.getElementById('regUsername').value.trim();
  const p = document.getElementById('regPassword').value;
  const p2 = document.getElementById('regPassword2').value;
  if (p !== p2) { document.getElementById('authError').textContent = 'Пароли не совпадают'; return; }
  fetch('/api/register', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({username:u, password:p}) })
  .then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    if (d.success) { currentUser = d.user; showMain(); addLog('Аккаунт создан: ' + u, 'success'); }
    else { document.getElementById('authError').textContent = d.error; }
  });
}
function doLogout() { fetch('/api/logout', { method:'POST' }).then(() => { currentUser = null; showAuth(); }); }

function sendMessage() {
  const r = document.getElementById('receiver').value;
  const m = document.getElementById('message').value;
  if (!r || !m) { addLog('Заполните все поля', 'error'); return; }
  fetch('/api/p2/send', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({receiver:r, message:m}) })
  .then(r2 => r2.json()).then(d => {
    addLog(d.success ? '✅ Отправлено' : '❌ Ошибка: ' + (d.error || ''), d.success ? 'success' : 'error');
    if (d.success) document.getElementById('message').value = '';
  });
}
function loadInbox() {
  fetch('/api/p2/inbox').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    const el = document.getElementById('inboxList');
    if (!el) return;
    if (!d.messages || !d.messages.length) { el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Пока пусто</div>'; return; }
    el.innerHTML = d.messages.slice().reverse().map(m => '<div class="list-item"><div><b>' + (m.sender || '?') + '</b><br><span style="color:var(--dim);font-size:0.85em">' + (m.message || '') + '</span></div><span style="color:var(--dim);font-size:0.7em">' + new Date(m.ts * 1000).toLocaleTimeString() + '</span></div>').join('');
  }).catch(() => {});
}

function checkTor() {
  addLog('Проверка Tor...', 'info');
  fetch('/api/tor/status').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    const el = document.getElementById('anonResult');
    if (d.available) { el.textContent = '🧅 Tor: Доступен'; addLog('Tor работает', 'success'); }
    else { el.textContent = '🧅 Tor: Не запущен'; addLog('Tor не найден', 'warn'); }
  });
}
function checkI2P() {
  addLog('Проверка I2P...', 'info');
  fetch('/api/i2p/status').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    const el = document.getElementById('anonResult');
    if (d.available) { el.textContent = '🌐 I2P: Доступен'; addLog('I2P работает', 'success'); }
    else { el.textContent = '🌐 I2P: Не запущен'; addLog('I2P не найден', 'warn'); }
  });
}

function scanWiFi() {
  addLog('Поиск Wi-Fi сетей...', 'info');
  document.getElementById('scanResult').textContent = 'Сканирование Wi-Fi...';
  fetch('/api/rf/scan', { method:'POST' }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    document.getElementById('scanResult').textContent = '📶 Wi-Fi: ' + (d.wifi || 0) + ' | 📱 BT: ' + (d.bt || 0);
    addLog('Wi-Fi найдено: ' + (d.wifi || 0), 'success');
  });
}
function scanBLE() {
  addLog('Поиск Bluetooth устройств (5 сек)...', 'info');
  document.getElementById('scanResult').textContent = 'Сканирование Bluetooth...';
  fetch('/api/ble/scan', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({duration:5}) }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    document.getElementById('scanResult').textContent = '📱 Найдено Bluetooth: ' + (d.count || 0);
    addLog('Bluetooth: ' + (d.count || 0) + ' устройств', 'success');
  });
}
function scanLTE() {
  addLog('Поиск сотовых сетей...', 'info');
  document.getElementById('scanResult').textContent = 'Сканирование сотовых...';
  fetch('/api/cellular/scan', { method:'POST' }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    const modem = (d.modem || []).length;
    const wifi = (d.via_wifi || []).length;
    document.getElementById('scanResult').textContent = '📡 Модем: ' + modem + ' | Через Wi-Fi: ' + wifi;
    addLog('Сотовые: ' + (modem + wifi), 'success');
  });
}

function checkBridge() {
  addLog('Проверка P2P моста...', 'info');
  fetch('/api/bridge/status').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    const b = d.broker || {};
    document.getElementById('bridgeResult').textContent = 'Реле-узлов: ' + (b.total_relays || 0) + ' | Доступно: ' + (b.available_relays || 0);
    addLog('P2P мост: ' + (b.total_relays || 0) + ' реле-узлов', 'success');
  });
}
function becomeVolunteer() {
  const region = document.getElementById('bridgeRegion').value || 'unknown';
  const capacity = parseInt(document.getElementById('bridgeCapacity').value) || 10;
  addLog('Регистрация реле-узла...', 'info');
  fetch('/api/bridge/volunteer', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({region, capacity}) }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    if (d.success) { document.getElementById('bridgeResult').textContent = '✅ Вы реле-узел! Лимит: ' + (d.relay.capacity || 0); addLog('Реле-узел запущен', 'success'); }
    else { document.getElementById('bridgeResult').textContent = '❌ Ошибка'; }
  });
}
function stopVolunteer() {
  addLog('Остановка реле-узла...', 'info');
  fetch('/api/bridge/stop', { method:'POST' }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    if (d.success) { document.getElementById('bridgeResult').textContent = 'Остановлен'; addLog('Реле-узел остановлен', 'success'); }
  });
}

function scanIndustrial() {
  const target = document.getElementById('industrialTarget').value.trim();
  if (!target) { addLog('Введите адрес', 'error'); return; }
  addLog('Поиск промышленных устройств: ' + target, 'info');
  document.getElementById('industrialResults').textContent = 'Сканирование...';
  fetch('/api/industrial/scan', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({target}) }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    if (d.success) {
      let out = [];
      for (const [proto, res] of Object.entries(d.results || {})) {
        out.push(proto + ': ' + (res.found ? '✅ найдено' : 'не найдено'));
      }
      document.getElementById('industrialResults').textContent = out.join(' | ');
      addLog('Промышленный скан завершён', 'success');
    }
  });
}

function generateQR() {
  fetch('/api/qr').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    if (d.success) {
      const img = document.getElementById('qrImage');
      img.src = d.qr_url + '?t=' + Date.now();
      img.style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('qrPlaceholder').style.display = 'none';
      addLog('QR-код готов', 'success');
    }
  });
}
function copyNodeId() {
  if (currentUser && navigator.clipboard) {
    navigator.clipboard.writeText(currentUser.node_id || '');
    addLog('ID скопирован', 'success');
  }
}

function addContactFromQR() {
  const raw = document.getElementById('qrImportData').value.trim();
  if (!raw) return;
  try {
    const q = JSON.parse(raw);
    fetch('/api/contacts', { method:'POST', headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'}, body:JSON.stringify({qr_data:q}) }).then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
      addLog(d.added ? '✅ Контакт добавлен' : 'Уже есть', d.added ? 'success' : 'warn');
      loadContacts();
    });
  } catch (e) { addLog('Неверный формат', 'error'); }
}
function removeContact(id) { fetch('/api/contacts/' + id, { method:'DELETE' }).then(() => loadContacts()); }
function loadContacts() {
  fetch('/api/contacts').then(r => r.json()).then(d => {
    const cs = d.contacts || [];
    document.getElementById('contactsCount').textContent = cs.length;
    document.getElementById('contactsList').innerHTML = cs.length
      ? cs.map(c => '<div class="list-item"><span>' + (c.username || '?') + ' <span style="color:var(--dim);font-size:0.8em">' + (c.node_id || '') + '</span></span><button class="del" onclick="removeContact(\'' + c.node_id + '\')">x</button></div>').join('')
      : '<div class="empty">Нет контактов</div>';
  });
}


async function loadAIStats() {
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/ai/stats');
        const d = await r.json();
        if (!d.success) {
            addLog('AI stats failed', 'error');
            return;
        }
        const sel = d.selector || {};
        const protos = d.protocols || [];
        const rec = d.recursion || {};

        const best = sel.best_protocol || '—';
        const bestScore = sel.best_score || 0;
        const bestEl = document.getElementById('aiBestName');
        if (bestEl) bestEl.textContent = best;
        const scoreEl = document.getElementById('aiBestScore');
        if (scoreEl) scoreEl.textContent = bestScore.toFixed(3);
        const barEl = document.getElementById('aiBestBar');
        if (barEl) barEl.style.width = Math.round(bestScore * 100) + '%';

        const sorted = [...protos].sort((a, b) =>
            (b.success_rate || 0) - (a.success_rate || 0));
        const medals = ['🥇', '🥈', '🥉'];
        const listEl = document.getElementById('aiTopList');
        if (listEl) {
            if (sorted.length > 0) {
                listEl.innerHTML = sorted.slice(0, 3).map((p, i) => {
                    const pct = Math.round((p.success_rate || 0) * 100);
                    return '<div class="ai-protocol-row">' +
                        '<span><span class="ai-medal">' + (medals[i] || '•') + '</span>' +
                        '<span class="ai-protocol-name">' + p.protocol + '</span></span>' +
                        '<span class="ai-protocol-score">' + pct + '% (' + p.attempts + 'x)</span>' +
                        '</div>';
                }).join('');
            } else {
                listEl.innerHTML = '<div class="empty" style="padding:10px">Нет данных</div>';
            }
        }

        const recEl = document.getElementById('aiRecords');
        if (recEl) recEl.textContent = sel.total_records || 0;
        const tgtEl = document.getElementById('aiTargets');
        if (tgtEl) tgtEl.textContent = sel.unique_targets || 0;
        const callsEl = document.getElementById('aiCalls');
        if (callsEl) callsEl.textContent = rec.total_calls || 0;
        const rateEl = document.getElementById('aiRate');
        if (rateEl) rateEl.textContent = Math.round((rec.success_rate || 0) * 100) + '%';

        addLog('AI: ' + best + ' score=' + bestScore.toFixed(2), 'success');
    } catch (e) {
        addLog('AI: ' + e, 'error');
    }
}

async function resetAI() {
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/ai/reset', { method: 'POST' });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            addLog('AI reset', 'success');
            loadAIStats();
        }
    } catch (e) {
        addLog('Reset: ' + e, 'error');
    }
}



async function addTrusted() {
    const host = document.getElementById('capsuleHostInput').value.trim();
    const label = document.getElementById('capsuleLabelInput').value.trim();
    if (!host) { addLog('Введите host', 'error'); return; }
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/capsule/trusted', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({host, label})
        });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            addLog('Добавлено: ' + host, 'success');
            loadCapsuleStats();
        } else {
            addLog('Ошибка: ' + (d.error || '?'), 'error');
        }
    } catch (e) { addLog('Trusted: ' + e, 'error'); }
}

async function removeTrusted(host) {
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/capsule/trusted', {
            method: 'DELETE',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({host})
        });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            addLog('Удалено: ' + host, 'success');
            loadCapsuleStats();
        }
    } catch (e) { addLog('Remove: ' + e, 'error'); }
}

async function deployAll() {
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/capsule/stats');
        const d = await r.json();
        const hosts = (d.stats && d.stats.trusted_hosts) || [];
        if (!hosts.length) { addLog('Нет доверенных устройств', 'warn'); return; }
        for (const h of hosts) {
            const r2 = await fetch('/api/capsule/deploy', {
                method: 'POST',
                headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
                body: JSON.stringify({host: h.host})
            });
            const d2 = await r2.json();
            addLog((d2.success ? '✅ ' : '❌ ') + h.host, d2.success ? 'success' : 'error');
        }
        loadCapsuleStats();
    } catch (e) { addLog('Deploy: ' + e, 'error'); }
}

async function loadCapsuleStats() {
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/capsule/stats');
        const d = await r.json();
        if (!d.success) return;
        const hosts = (d.stats && d.stats.trusted_hosts) || [];
        const el = document.getElementById('capsuleList');
        if (hosts.length) {
            el.innerHTML = hosts.map(h =>
                '<div class="list-item"><span>' + (h.label || h.host) +
                ' <span style="color:var(--dim);font-size:0.8em">' + h.host + '</span></span>' +
                '<button class="del" onclick="removeTrusted(\'' + h.host + '\')">×</button></div>'
            ).join('');
        } else {
            el.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Нет доверенных устройств</div>';
        }
        const dep = (d.stats && d.stats.deployer) || {};
        document.getElementById('capsuleStats').textContent =
            'Попыток: ' + (dep.attempts || 0) +
            ' | Успех: ' + (dep.success || 0) +
            ' | Отказ: ' + (dep.failed || 0);
        addLog('Capsule stats loaded', 'success');
    } catch (e) { addLog('Capsule: ' + e, 'error'); }
}



async function killCapsules() {
    if (!confirm('Убить все капсулы и заблокировать автозапуск?')) return;
    addLog('KILL: останавливаю капсулы...', 'warn');
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/capsule/kill', { method: 'POST' });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            addLog('Убито процессов: ' + d.killed + ', автозапуск заблокирован', 'success');
        } else {
            addLog('KILL error: ' + (d.error || '?'), 'error');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        addLog('KILL: ' + e, 'error');
    }
}



async function deployAllNodes() {
    if (!confirm('Попробовать развернуть капсулу на ВСЕ найденные узлы?')) return;
    addLog('Deploy to ALL nodes...', 'warn');
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/capsule/deploy_all', { method: 'POST' });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            addLog('Deployed: ' + d.deployed +
                   ' | Failed: ' + d.failed +
                   ' | Skipped: ' + d.skipped, 'success');
            // Show results
            const el = document.getElementById('capsuleList');
            if (el) {
                let out = '<div style="margin-top:10px">';
                out += '<b>Результаты (' + (d.results || []).length + '):</b><br>';
                const sorted = (d.results || []).sort((a, b) => {
                    const order = {deployed: 0, failed: 1, skip: 2};
                    return (order[a.status] || 9) - (order[b.status] || 9);
                });
                for (const res of sorted) {
                    const cls = res.status === 'deployed' ? 'deployed' :
                                res.status === 'failed' ? 'failed' : 'skip';
                    const icon = res.status === 'deployed' ? '✅' :
                                 res.status === 'failed' ? '❌' : '⚠️';
                    const reason = res.skip_reason ? ' (' + res.skip_reason + ')' : '';
                    out += '<div class="deploy-result ' + cls + '">' +
                           icon + ' ' + (res.type || '?') +
                           ' @ ' + (res.target || 'unknown') +
                           reason + '</div>';
                }
                out += '</div>';
                el.innerHTML = out + el.innerHTML;
            }
        } else {
            addLog('Deploy all error: ' + (d.error || '?'), 'error');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        addLog('Deploy all: ' + e, 'error');
    }
}



async function scanRouter() {
    const ip = document.getElementById('routerScanIp').value.trim();
    if (!ip) { addLog('Введите IP роутера', 'error'); return; }
    addLog('Scan router: ' + ip, 'info');
    const el = document.getElementById('routerScanResult');
    el.textContent = 'Сканирую...';
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/router/scan', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({ ip })
        });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            const i = d.info;
            el.innerHTML =
                'Vendor: ' + i.vendor + '<br>' +
                'Firmware: ' + i.firmware + '<br>' +
                'SSH: ' + (i.ssh_open ? '✅' : '❌') +
                ' HTTP: ' + (i.http_open ? '✅' : '❌') +
                ' HTTPS: ' + (i.https_open ? '✅' : '❌') + '<br>' +
                'Method: ' + i.deploy_method + '<br>' +
                'Can deploy: ' + (i.can_deploy ? '✅ YES' : '❌ NO') + '<br>' +
                'Notes: ' + i.notes;
            addLog('Router: ' + i.vendor + ' / ' + i.firmware, 'success');
        } else {
            el.textContent = 'Error: ' + d.error;
        }
    } catch (e) {
        addLog('Router scan: ' + e, 'error');
    }
}



async function networkSweep() {
    if (!confirm('Пропинговать всю подсеть 192.168.x.1-254?\nЭто может занять 10-15 секунд.')) return;
    addLog('Ping sweep 192.168.x.x ...', 'warn');
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/network/sweep', {
            method: 'POST',
            headers: {'Content-Type': 'application/json'},
            body: JSON.stringify({})
        });
        const d = await r.json();
        if (d.success) {
            addLog('Sweep: alive=' + d.alive + ', devices=' + d.devices_found, 'success');
            const el = document.getElementById('capsuleList');
            if (el) {
                let out = '<div style="margin-top:10px">';
                out += '<b>Найдено устройств (' + d.devices_found + '):</b><br>';
                for (const dev of (d.devices || [])) {
                    out += '<div class="deploy-result deployed">✅ ' +
                           dev.ip + ' (' + dev.type + ') ' +
                           dev.mac + '</div>';
                }
                out += '</div>';
                el.innerHTML = out + el.innerHTML;
            }
        } else {
            addLog('Sweep error: ' + (d.error || '?'), 'error');
        }
    } catch (e) {
        addLog('Sweep: ' + e, 'error');
    }
}



let autoDeployEnabled = true;

async function toggleI2P() {
    const checked = document.getElementById('toggleI2P').checked;
    addLog('I2P toggle: ' + checked, 'info');
    // I2P уже запускается в get_net() — просто показать статус
    try {
        const r = await fetch('/api/i2p/status');
        const d = await r.json();
        addLog('I2P: ' + (d.available ? 'AVAILABLE' : 'not running'), d.available ? 'success' : 'warn');
    } catch (e) {}
}

async function toggleAutoDeploy() {
    autoDeployEnabled = document.getElementById('toggleAutoDeploy').checked;
    const el = document.getElementById('autoDeployStatus');
    if (el) el.textContent = 'Auto-deploy: ' + (autoDeployEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF');
    addLog('Auto-deploy: ' + (autoDeployEnabled ? 'ON' : 'OFF'), 'info');
}
